
============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
apply_diffusion_to_vn(
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_v1,
  primal_normal_vert_v2,
  z_nabla2_e,
  inv_vert_vert_length,
  inv_primal_edge_length,
  area_edge,
  kh_smag_e,
  diff_multfac_vn,
  nudgecoeff_e,
  vn,
  nudgezone_diff,
  fac_bdydiff_v,
  start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
  limited_area,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_global_to_vn = λ(
    area_edge,
    kh_smag_e,
    z_nabla2_e,
    z_nabla4_e2,
    diff_multfac_vn,
    vn
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
               (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                 vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                       area_edge,
                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0, z_nabla2_e),
                         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                           (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(diff_multfac_vn, z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0), area_edge
                         )
                       )
                     )
               )
             ))(__tuple_tmp_0[1]))(__tuple_tmp_0[0]))(
      (λ(__val_3e5cfc464786f1dd) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_3e5cfc464786f1dd[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_3e5cfc464786f1dd[1])})(
        {kh_smag_e, z_nabla4_e2}
      )
    );
  _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_to_vn = λ(
    area_edge,
    kh_smag_e,
    z_nabla2_e,
    z_nabla4_e2,
    diff_multfac_vn,
    nudgecoeff_e,
    vn,
    nudgezone_diff
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(nudgezone_diff_wpᐞ0) → 
                (λ(area_edge_broadcastᐞ0) → 
                   (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
                     (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                       vn,
                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                         area_edge,
                         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                           (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                             (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → maximum(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
                               (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(nudgezone_diff_wpᐞ0, nudgecoeff_e), kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0
                             ), z_nabla2_e
                           ),
                           (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                             (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(area_edge_broadcastᐞ0, diff_multfac_vn), z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0
                           )
                         )
                       )
                     )
                   ))(broadcast(area_edge, {Edgeₕ, Kᵥ})))(__tuple_tmp_0[2]))(__tuple_tmp_0[1]))(__tuple_tmp_0[0]))(
      (λ(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[1]),
          (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[2])})(
        {kh_smag_e, z_nabla4_e2, nudgezone_diff}
      )
    );
  _apply_nabla2_to_vn_in_lateral_boundary = λ(z_nabla2_e, area_edge, vn, fac_bdydiff_v) → 
    (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
        vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(area_edge, fac_bdydiff_v), z_nabla2_e
            )
      )
    );
  _calculate_nabla4 = λ(
    u_vert,
    v_vert,
    primal_normal_vert_v1,
    primal_normal_vert_v2,
    z_nabla2_e,
    inv_vert_vert_length,
    inv_primal_edge_length
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(u_vert_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(v_vert_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(nabv_tang_vpᐞ0) → 
                (λ(nabv_norm_vpᐞ0) → 
                   (λ(__tuple_tmp_1) → 
                      (λ(nabv_tang_wpᐞ0) → 
                         (λ(nabv_norm_wpᐞ0) → 
                            (λ(z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0) → (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0))(
                              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                                4.0,
                                (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                                    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                                      nabv_norm_wpᐞ0, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(2.0, z_nabla2_e)
                                    ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(inv_vert_vert_length, inv_vert_vert_length)
                                  ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                                         nabv_tang_wpᐞ0, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(2.0, z_nabla2_e)
                                       ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(inv_primal_edge_length, inv_primal_edge_length)
                                     )
                                )
                              )
                            ))(__tuple_tmp_1[1]))(__tuple_tmp_1[0]))(
                     (λ(__val_353b8100fdb3f8f5) → 
                        {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_353b8100fdb3f8f5[0]),
                         (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_353b8100fdb3f8f5[1])})(
                       {nabv_tang_vpᐞ0, nabv_norm_vpᐞ0}
                     )
                   ))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(
                        (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                          (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(__it)))(u_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                         (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(2, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v1)),
                              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 2ₒ⟫(__it)))(v_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                         (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(2, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v2))
                            ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(__it)))(u_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                          (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(3, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v1))
                          ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 3ₒ⟫(__it)))(v_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                        (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(3, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v2))
                        )
                      )))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(
                            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(__it)))(u_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                             (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(0, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v1)),
                                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 0ₒ⟫(__it)))(v_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                             (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(0, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v2))
                                ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(u_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                              (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(1, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v1))
                              ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(v_vert_wpᐞ0),
                                                                            (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(1, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v2))
                            )
                          )))(__tuple_tmp_0[1]))(__tuple_tmp_0[0]))(
      (λ(__val_7d0a01247e35a640) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_7d0a01247e35a640[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_7d0a01247e35a640[1])})(
        {u_vert, v_vert}
      )
    );
  _apply_diffusion_to_vn = λ(
    u_vert,
    v_vert,
    primal_normal_vert_v1,
    primal_normal_vert_v2,
    z_nabla2_e,
    inv_vert_vert_length,
    inv_primal_edge_length,
    area_edge,
    kh_smag_e,
    diff_multfac_vn,
    nudgecoeff_e,
    vn,
    nudgezone_diff,
    fac_bdydiff_v,
    start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
    limited_area
  ) → 
    (λ(z_nabla4_e2ᐞ0) → 
       (λ(vnᐞ0) → vnᐞ0)(
         if   limited_area
         then concat_where(
                Edgeₕ >= start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
                _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_to_vn(
                  area_edge, kh_smag_e, z_nabla2_e, z_nabla4_e2ᐞ0, diff_multfac_vn, nudgecoeff_e, vn, nudgezone_diff
                ),
                _apply_nabla2_to_vn_in_lateral_boundary(z_nabla2_e, area_edge, vn, fac_bdydiff_v)
              )
         else concat_where(Edgeₕ >= start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
                           _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_global_to_vn(
                             area_edge, kh_smag_e, z_nabla2_e, z_nabla4_e2ᐞ0, diff_multfac_vn, vn
                           ),
                           vn)
       ))(_calculate_nabla4(u_vert,
                            v_vert,
                            primal_normal_vert_v1,
                            primal_normal_vert_v2,
                            z_nabla2_e,
                            inv_vert_vert_length,
                            inv_primal_edge_length));
  vn @ u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
       ← _apply_diffusion_to_vn(u_vert,
                                v_vert,
                                primal_normal_vert_v1,
                                primal_normal_vert_v2,
                                z_nabla2_e,
                                inv_vert_vert_length,
                                inv_primal_edge_length,
                                area_edge,
                                kh_smag_e,
                                diff_multfac_vn,
                                nudgecoeff_e,
                                vn,
                                nudgezone_diff,
                                fac_bdydiff_v,
                                start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
                                limited_area);
}

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING FUNDEFS ===
============================================================
apply_diffusion_to_vn(
  u_vert,
  v_vert,
  primal_normal_vert_v1,
  primal_normal_vert_v2,
  z_nabla2_e,

  vn,
  nudgezone_diff,

  start_2nd_nudge_line_idx_e,
  limited_area,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_global_to_vn = λ(
    area_edge,

    diff_multfac_vn,
    vn
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0, z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
              vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                    area_edge,
                    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0, z_nabla2_e),
                      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                        (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(diff_multfac_vn, z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0), area_edge

          ))(__tuple_tmp_0[0], __tuple_tmp_0[1]))(
      (λ(__val_3e5cfc464786f1dd) →

        {kh_smag_e, z_nabla4_e2}
      )
    );
  _apply_nabla2_and_nabla4_to_vn = λ(
    area_edge,

    z_nabla2_e,
    z_nabla4_e2,
    diff_multfac_vn,
    nudgecoeff_e,
    vn,
    nudgezone_diff
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(kh_smag_e_wpᐞ0, z_nabla4_e2_wpᐞ0, nudgezone_diff_wpᐞ0, area_edge_broadcastᐞ0) → 
          (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(

              vn,
              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                area_edge,
                (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → maximum(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(

                  ),
                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(

                  )
                )
              )
            )
          ))(__tuple_tmp_0[0], __tuple_tmp_0[1], __tuple_tmp_0[2], broadcast(area_edge, {Edgeₕ, Kᵥ})))(
      (λ(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[1]),
          (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(__val_2d07ee9ae56c5adc[2])})(

      )
    );
  _apply_nabla2_to_vn_in_lateral_boundary = λ(z_nabla2_e, area_edge, vn, fac_bdydiff_v) → 
    (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(

    );
  _calculate_nabla4 = λ(

    inv_primal_edge_length
  ) →

                         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(

               )

                    ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))((⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(v_vert_wpᐞ0),

                  )

                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(

                                     (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2C2Vₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(__ct_el_11), (⇑(λ(it) → list_get(1, ·it)))(primal_normal_vert_v1)

                                 )
                            )
                          )

                      ), (⇑(λ(__arg0___, __arg1_) → ·__arg0___ × ·__arg1_))(inv_primal_edge_length, inv_primal_edge_length)

                          ), float64

                            + cast_(·⟪_OffJDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64) × list_get(0, ·primal_normal_vert_v2)
                            + cast_(·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(u_vert), float64) × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_v1)
                            + cast_(·⟪_OffIDimₒ, 1ₒ, _OffKolorₒ, -2ₒ⟫(v_vert), float64) × list_get(1, ·primal_normal_vert_v2),
                            float64
                          ), float64
                        ) - _cs_51) × (_cs_53 × _cs_53)) × 4.0, float64
                  ))(·z_nabla2_e × 2.0, ·inv_vert_vert_length, ·inv_primal_edge_length),
             c⟨ IDimₕ: [4, 508[, JDimₕ: [4, 508[, Kolorₕ: [2, 3[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
           )(u_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_v1,
             v_vert,
             primal_normal_vert_v2,
             z_nabla2_e,
             inv_vert_vert_length,
             inv_primal_edge_length)
         );
}
